Um... well... I guess maybe sometimes I get a little stuck? But it's not like I'm frozen or anything bad like that.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um, well... I guess I might zone out sometimes? But, you know, just for a minute or two, like anyone does when they're tired after a long shift. 

I definitely don't stand around like a statue or stare at the wall for hours or anything like that. I know you're probably asking because of what I said earlier about pausing by the forklift with my arms up, but that's really just me being careful and trying to direct traffic, not me getting stuck in a weird pose. I wouldn't want you to think I was just standing around doing nothing while everyone else is working so hard. I try to be a really good worker.

So, no, I don't hold weird postures for a long time. I just take normal little breaks to catch my breath. 

Does that sound okay? I hope I'm explaining things clearly enough for you. I'm really trying to be as helpful as I can.

Oh, um... no. Definitely not outside of work. Like, if I'm at the grocery store or just out and about, I wouldn't ever do anything like that.
